2026-10-25T03:57:10.3345982Z     def _is_language_support_installed(self, lang):
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3346795Z         """Is the support for the specified language installed?
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3347572Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3348050Z         The language is considered to be supported if we are not
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3348954Z         able to determine the supported locales due to missing tools.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3349614Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3350016Z         :param lang: a value for the LANG locale variable
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3351206Z         :return: False if the locale is known to be not supported, otherwise True
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3352051Z         """
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3352646Z         try:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3353336Z             output = execWithCapture("locale", ["-a"], root=self._sysroot)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3354183Z         except OSError as e:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3354915Z             log.warning("Couldn't get supported locales: %s", e)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3355692Z             return True
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3356339Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3356805Z         match = find_best_locale_match(lang, output.splitlines())
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3357446Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3357915Z         log.debug("The '%s' locale matched '%s'.", lang, match)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3361210Z         return bool(match)

2026-10-25T03:57:10.3367581Z             with open(fpath, "w") as fobj:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3368313Z                 fobj.write('LANG="{}"\n'.format(lang))

2026-10-25T03:57:10.3417081Z def write_x_configuration(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, switch_options, x_conf_dir_path, root):
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3418197Z     """Write X keyboard layout configuration to the configuration files.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3418829Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3419292Z     :param localed_wrapper: instance of systemd-localed service wrapper
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3420116Z     :type localed_wrapper: LocaledWrapper
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3421209Z     :param x_layouts: list of X layout specifications

2026-10-25T03:57:10.3424124Z     :param x_conf_dir_path: path to directory holding X Layouts configuration

2026-10-25T03:57:10.3425560Z     :param root: root path of the configured system
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3426211Z     :type root: str
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3426699Z     """
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3427023Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3427299Z     errors = []
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3427772Z     try:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3428284Z         if not os.path.isdir(x_conf_dir_path):

2026-10-25T03:57:10.3432232Z

2026-10-25T03:57:10.3433190Z         # Writing to a different root, we need to save these values, so that
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3434050Z         # we can restore them when we have the file written out.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3434920Z         saved_layouts_variants = localed_wrapper.get_layouts_variants()

2026-10-25T03:57:10.3436226Z

2026-10-25T03:57:10.3437468Z     # also generates a new conf file.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3438179Z     localed_wrapper.set_layouts(x_layouts, switch_options)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3438736Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3439012Z     if root != "/":
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3439656Z         # Make sure the target directory exists under the given root.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3440954Z         rooted_xconf_dir = os.path.normpath(root + "/" + x_conf_dir_path)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3441776Z         try:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3442313Z             if not os.path.isdir(rooted_xconf_dir):
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3443011Z                 os.makedirs(rooted_xconf_dir)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3443685Z         except OSError:

2026-10-25T03:57:10.3444821Z

2026-10-25T03:57:10.3447010Z         rooted_xconf_file_path = os.path.normpath(root + "/" + xconf_file_path)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3447646Z         try:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3448092Z             shutil.copy2(xconf_file_path, rooted_xconf_file_path)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3448871Z         except OSError as ioerr:

2026-10-25T03:57:10.3453465Z def write_vc_configuration(vc_keymap, root):
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3453831Z     """Write virtual console keyboard mapping configuration.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3454072Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3454224Z     :param vc_keymap: virtual console keyboard mapping name
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3454555Z     :type vc_keymap: str
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3454836Z     :param root: root path of the configured system
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3455144Z     :type root: str
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3455344Z     """
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3455528Z     try:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3455772Z         fpath = os.path.normpath(root + VC_CONF_FILE_PATH)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3456007Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3456174Z         # "eurlatgr" may not be the best choice for all the languages,
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3456603Z         # for example, Russian, Urdu, Serbian, Sindhi and a few more.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3457037Z         # refer: https://bugzilla.redhat.com/show_bug.cgi?id=1919486
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3457396Z         # assuming that LANG is set by now.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3457733Z         vc_fonts = get_locale_console_fonts(os.environ["LANG"])
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3458109Z         vc_font = vc_fonts[0] if vc_fonts else DEFAULT_VC_FONT
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3458339Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3458440Z         with open(fpath, "w") as fobj:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3458735Z             fobj.write('KEYMAP="%s"\n' % vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3458941Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3459124Z             # systemd now defaults to a font that cannot display non-ascii
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3459548Z             # characters, so we have to tell it to use a better one
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3459896Z             fobj.write('FONT="%s"\n' % vc_font)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3460101Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3460195Z     except OSError as e:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3461166Z         msg = "Cannot write vconsole configuration file: {}".format(e.strerror)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3461914Z         raise KeyboardInstallationError(msg) from e

2026-10-25T03:57:10.3579686Z class CompositorLocaledWrapper(LocaledWrapperBase):
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3580743Z     """Localed wrapper class which is used to control compositor.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3581207Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3581617Z     It adds support for layout selection and reactions on the compositor system changes.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3582325Z     """
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3582504Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3582650Z     def __init__(self):
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3583026Z         super().__init__()
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3583286Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3583461Z         self._user_layouts_variants = []
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3583942Z         self._last_layouts_variants = []
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3584433Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3584650Z         self.compositor_layouts_changed = Signal()
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3585246Z         self.compositor_selected_layout_changed = Signal()
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3585638Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3585827Z         # to reflect updates from the compositor
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3586562Z         self._localed_proxy.PropertiesChanged.connect(self._on_properties_changed)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3587117Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3587475Z     def _on_properties_changed(self, interface, changed_props, invalid_props):
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3588290Z         if "X11Layout" in changed_props or "X11Variant" in changed_props:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3589171Z             layouts_variants = self._from_localed_format(changed_props["X11Layout"].get_string(),
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3590039Z                                                          changed_props["X11Variant"].get_string())
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3591542Z             # This part is a bit tricky. The signal processing here means that compositor has
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3592537Z             # changed current layouts configuration. This could happen for multiple reasons:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3593325Z             # - user changed the layout in compositor
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3593874Z             # - Anaconda set the layout to compositor
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3594495Z             # - any other magic logic for compositor (we just don't know)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3595071Z             #
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3595448Z             # The question is how we should behave:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3596165Z             # - we don't want to take compositor layouts to Anaconda because that will change
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3596928Z             #   what user will have in the installed system.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3597672Z             # - we don't want to force our layouts to compositor because that would forbid user
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3598513Z             #   to change compositor layout when Anaconda runs in background
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3599109Z             #
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3599717Z             # The best shot seems to just signal out that the layout has changed and nothing else.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3600291Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3600935Z             # layouts has changed in compositor, always emit this signal
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3601732Z             log.debug("Localed layouts has changed. Last known: '%s' current: '%s'",
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3602487Z                       self._last_layouts_variants, layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3603154Z             self.compositor_layouts_changed.emit(layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3603599Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3603832Z             # check if last selected variant has changed
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3604350Z             # nothing is selected in compositor
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3604895Z             if not layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3605296Z                 log.warning("Compositor layouts not set.")
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3605690Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit("")
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3606211Z             # we don't know last used layouts
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3606740Z             elif not self._last_layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3607346Z                 log.debug("Compositor selected layout is different. "
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3608030Z                           "Missing information about last selected layouts.")

2026-10-25T03:57:10.3642952Z     def _set_layouts(self, layouts_variants, options=None, convert=False):
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3643620Z         """Set a new layouts for compositor.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3643946Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3644240Z         Extension of the parent code with compositor specific code.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3644827Z         """
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3645406Z         # If options are not set let's use from a system so we don't change the system settings
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3646121Z         if options is None:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3646543Z             options = self.options
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3646969Z             log.debug(
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3647622Z                 "Keyboard layouts for compositor are missing options. Use compositor options: %s",
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3648378Z                 options,
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3648747Z             )
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3648943Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3649355Z         # Disable keyboard layout switching by keyboard shortcut as it is hard to make this
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3650542Z         # working from the system to localed. The current layout is in general problematic to
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3651465Z         # decide.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3651831Z         log.debug(
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3652555Z             "Disable keyboard layouts switching shortcut to compositor from the options: '%s'",
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3653310Z             options,
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3653660Z         )
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3654340Z         options = list(filter(lambda x: not x.startswith("grp:"), options))
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3654828Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3655008Z         # store configuration from user

2026-10-25T03:57:10.3655991Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3656178Z     def select_layout(self, layout_variant):
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3656754Z         """Select layout from the list of current layouts set.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3657153Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3657517Z         This will search for the given layout variant in the list and move it as first
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3658348Z         in the list. The first layout in systemd is taken as the used one.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3658809Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3659158Z         :param layout_variant: The layout to set, with format "layout (variant)"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3659980Z             (e.g. "cz (qwerty)")
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3660820Z         :type layout_variant: str
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3661366Z         :return: If the keyboard layout was activated
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3661880Z         :rtype: bool
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3662235Z         """
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3662705Z         # ignore compositor layouts but force Anaconda configuration
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3663340Z         layouts = self._user_layouts_variants
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3663680Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3663815Z         try:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3664265Z             new_layouts = self._shift_list(layouts, layout_variant)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3664854Z             self._set_layouts(new_layouts)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3665314Z             return True
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3665691Z         except ValueError:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3666277Z             log.warning("Can't set layout: '%s' as first to the current set: %s",
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3666959Z                         layout_variant, layouts)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3667451Z             return False
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3667686Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3667834Z     @staticmethod
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3668261Z     def _shift_list(source_layouts, value_to_first):
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3668981Z         """Helper method to reorder list of layouts and move one as first in the list.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3669515Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3669886Z         We should preserve the ordering just shift items from start of the list to the
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3670811Z         end in the same order.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3671081Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3671292Z         When we want to set 2nd as first in this list:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3671798Z         ["cz", "es", "us"]
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3672216Z         The result should be:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3672642Z         ["es", "us", "cz"]
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3672899Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3673160Z         So the compositor has the same next layout as Anaconda.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3673587Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3673928Z         :raises: ValueError: if the list is small or the layout is not inside
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3674567Z         """
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3674972Z         value_id = source_layouts.index(value_to_first)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3675753Z         new_list = source_layouts[value_id:len(source_layouts)] + source_layouts[0:value_id]
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3676472Z         return new_list
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3676717Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3676895Z     def select_next_layout(self):
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3677469Z         """Select (make it first) next layout for compositor.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3677887Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3679315Z         Find current compositor layout in the list of defined layouts and set next to it as
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3680732Z         current (first) for compositor. We need to have user defined list because compositor
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3681801Z         layouts will change with the selection. Store this list when user is setting configuration
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3682697Z         to compositor. This list must not change ordering.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3683214Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3683563Z         :param user_layouts: List of layouts selected by user in Anaconda.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3684426Z         :type user_layouts: [str]
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3684997Z         :return: If switch was successful True otherwise False
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3685564Z         :rtype: bool
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3685933Z         """
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3686340Z         current_layout = self.current_layout_variant
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3686870Z         layout_id = 0
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3687104Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3687291Z         if not self._user_layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3688043Z             log.error("Can't switch next layout - user defined keyboard layout is not present!")
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3688782Z             return False
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3689034Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3689189Z         # find next layout
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3689685Z         for i, v in enumerate(self._user_layouts_variants):
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3690235Z             if v == current_layout:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3691136Z                 layout_id = i + 1
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3691624Z                 layout_id %= len(self._user_layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3692015Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3692149Z         try:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3692610Z             new_layouts = self._shift_list(self._user_layouts_variants,
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3693302Z                                            self._user_layouts_variants[layout_id])
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3693875Z             self._set_layouts(new_layouts)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3694321Z             return True
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3694700Z         except ValueError:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3695303Z             log.warning("Can't set next keyboard layout %s", self._user_layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3695981Z             return False

2026-10-25T03:57:10.3897543Z from pyanaconda.anaconda_loggers import get_module_logger
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3898284Z from pyanaconda.core.constants import DEFAULT_KEYBOARD
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3899153Z from pyanaconda.modules.localization.live_keyboard import get_live_keyboard_instance
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3899778Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3899942Z log = get_module_logger(__name__)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3900241Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3900246Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3901025Z def get_missing_keyboard_configuration(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap):
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3901805Z     """Get keyboard configuration if not set by user.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3902174Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3902340Z     Algorithm works as this:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3902851Z     1. Check if full keyboard configuration is already set
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3903396Z        -> return these
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3903906Z     2. If no X layouts, check if they can be obtained from live system
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3904520Z        -> read X layouts
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3904961Z     3a. If still no configuration is present
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3905608Z        -> use DEFAULT_KEYBOARD as console layout and fall through to...
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3906380Z     3b. If only one of the keyboard layout or virtual console keymap is set
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3907039Z        -> convert one to the other by localed
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3907376Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3907697Z     :param localed_wrapper: instance of systemd-localed service wrapper
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3908358Z     :type localed_wrapper: LocaledWrapper
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3908898Z     :param x_layouts: list of X layout specifications
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3909428Z     :type x_layouts: list(str)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3909939Z     :param vc_keymap: virtual console keyboard mapping name
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3910822Z     :type vc_keymap: str
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3911335Z     :returns: tuple of X layouts and VC keyboard settings
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3911912Z     :rtype: (list(str), str))
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3912319Z     """
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3912658Z     if vc_keymap and x_layouts:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3913352Z         log.debug("Keyboard layouts and virtual console keymap already set - nothing to do")
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3914282Z         return x_layouts, vc_keymap
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3914590Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3914793Z     live_keyboard = get_live_keyboard_instance()
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3915549Z     # layouts are not set by user, we should take a look for live configuration if available
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3916280Z     if not x_layouts and live_keyboard:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3916896Z         log.debug("Keyboard configuration from Live system is available")
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3917594Z         x_layouts = live_keyboard.read_keyboard_layouts()
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3917975Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3918146Z     if not vc_keymap and not x_layouts:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3918880Z         log.debug("Using default value %s for missing virtual console keymap", DEFAULT_KEYBOARD)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3919633Z         vc_keymap = DEFAULT_KEYBOARD
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3919929Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3920257Z     if not vc_keymap or not x_layouts:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3921270Z         x_layouts, vc_keymap = _resolve_missing_by_conversion(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3921904Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3922064Z     return x_layouts, vc_keymap
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3922340Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3922346Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3922692Z def _resolve_missing_by_conversion(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap):
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3923343Z     if not vc_keymap:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3923816Z         vc_keymap = localed_wrapper.convert_layouts(x_layouts)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3924591Z         log.debug("Missing virtual console keymap value %s converted from %s X layouts",
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3925344Z                   vc_keymap, x_layouts)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3925807Z     if not x_layouts:

2026-10-25T03:57:10.3954165Z # (this is insecure and should not be used in prodcuction):

2026-10-25T03:57:10.3957612Z     case "${option}"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3957989Z         in
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3958352Z         t)THEME_ID=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3958803Z         r)WEBUI_REMOTE=${OPTARG};;

2026-10-25T03:57:10.3959901Z        exit 1 ;;
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3960236Z     esac
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3960781Z done
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3960959Z

2026-10-25T03:57:10.3964870Z # Function to setup user-related environment
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3965369Z setup_user_environment() {
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3965911Z     # PKEXEC_UID is the uid for "gnome-initial-setup" or "liveuser"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3966564Z     # depending on how the installer gets started.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3967060Z     #
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3967580Z     # It's unset on non-live-images, so we just use the current user then (root)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3968261Z     if [ -n "${PKEXEC_UID:-}" ]; then
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3968800Z       # If PKEXEC_UID is set, use the user associated with it
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3969400Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(id -n -u "${PKEXEC_UID}")
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3969880Z     else
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3970908Z       # Try to find another user who is not root and has /usr/bin/bash as their shell
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3971971Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(grep '/usr/bin/bash' /etc/passwd | grep -v '^root' | cut -d: -f1 | head -n 1)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3972598Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3972903Z       # If no such user is found, use the current user running the script
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3973542Z       if [ -z "${INSTALLER_USER:-}" ]; then
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3974022Z         INSTALLER_USER=$(id -un)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3974430Z       fi
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3974735Z     fi
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3974930Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3975135Z     # Get the home directory of the selected user
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3975945Z     HOME_DIR=$(grep "^$INSTALLER_USER:" /etc/passwd | cut -d: -f6)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3976384Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3976643Z     # Check if the home directory exists, otherwise create it
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3977214Z     if [ ! -d "$HOME_DIR" ]; then
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3977799Z       echo "The home directory $HOME_DIR does not exist. Creating it." >&2
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3978430Z       mkdir -p "$HOME_DIR"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3978846Z       chown "$INSTALLER_USER" "$HOME_DIR"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3979289Z     else
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3979690Z       echo "Home directory $HOME_DIR already exists." >&2
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3980202Z     fi
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3980601Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3980843Z     # Return the user and home directory for further use
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3981389Z     echo "$INSTALLER_USER"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3981774Z }

2026-10-25T03:57:10.3982267Z get_default_browser() {
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3983010Z     # Read the default browser from the anaconda runtime configuration file but wait till it's available
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3984002Z     # FIXME: Related to: https://issues.redhat.com/browse/INSTALLER-4223
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3984633Z     timeout=60
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3984972Z     elapsed=0
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3985170Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3985383Z     while [ ! -f /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf ]; do
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3985932Z         if [ "$elapsed" -ge "$timeout" ]; then
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3986596Z             echo "Timed out waiting for anaconda.conf to be available." >&2
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3987209Z             exit 1
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3987421Z         fi
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3987622Z         sleep 1
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3987848Z         elapsed=$((elapsed + 1))
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3988314Z     done
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3988512Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3988986Z     # FIXME: Should be provided as an argument to this script instead of grepping the config file
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3990053Z     DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(grep '^webui_web_engine =' /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf | cut -d'=' -f2)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3991268Z     if [ -n "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" ]; then
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3991898Z         echo "Default browser set in anaconda.conf: $DEFAULT_BROWSER" >&2
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3992754Z         DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" | sed 's/^[[:space:]]*//;s/[[:space:]]*$//')
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3993502Z         echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3993928Z     else
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3994446Z         echo "No default browser set in anaconda.conf, using firefox." >&2
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3995090Z         echo "firefox"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3995464Z     fi
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3995781Z }

2026-10-25T03:57:10.4041578Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4041717Z     STRING=$1
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4042083Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"

2026-10-25T03:57:10.4045226Z     printf '\n'
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4045575Z     return $rc
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4045930Z }
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4046099Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4046385Z # verbose ->> very (very!) old bootup look (prior to RHL-6.0?)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4046977Z # color ->> default bootup look
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4047535Z # other ->> default bootup look without ANSI colors or positioning
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4048115Z BOOTUP=color
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4048472Z # Column to start "[  OK  ]" label in:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4048910Z RES_COL=60
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4049272Z # terminal sequence to move to that column:

2026-10-25T03:57:10.4051451Z SETCOLOR_SUCCESS="printf \\033[1;32m"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4052050Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'failure' (bright red):
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4052748Z SETCOLOR_FAILURE="printf \\033[1;31m"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4053341Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'warning' (bright yellow):
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4053957Z SETCOLOR_WARNING="printf \\033[1;33m"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4054477Z # Terminal sequence to reset to the default color:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4055019Z SETCOLOR_NORMAL="printf \\033[0;39m"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4055328Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4055496Z # NOTE: /dev/ttyS* is serial console.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4056093Z # 'not a tty' is reported when executed under systemd service units
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4056829Z # where /dev/null is associated with standard input
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4057241Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4057424Z test_tty=$(LC_ALL=C tty)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4058065Z case "$test_tty" in
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4058470Z     /dev/ttyS* | 'not a tty')
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4058905Z         BOOTUP=serial

2026-10-25T03:57:10.4076120Z }
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4076307Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4076458Z fscheck() {
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4076811Z     local fstype="$1"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4077212Z     local fs="$2"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4077577Z     local d="${3:-$2}"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4077966Z     umount "$fs" >/dev/null 2>&1 || :
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4078408Z     case "$fstype" in
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4078768Z         ext[432])
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4079111Z             printf '\n'
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4079514Z             flock "$d" e2fsck -yfv "$fs" || :
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4079971Z             ;;
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4080298Z         vfat|msdos)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4080679Z             printf '\n'
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4080863Z             flock "$d" fsck.fat -avVw "$fs"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4081006Z             ;;
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4081147Z         btrfs)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4081380Z             printf '# btrfs check -p --repair --force'
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4081628Z             flock "$d" btrfs check -p --repair --force "$fs"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4081777Z             ;;
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4081921Z         xfs)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4082093Z             printf '\n# xfs_repair -v'
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4082274Z             flock "$d" xfs_repair -v "$fs"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4082415Z             ;;
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4082546Z         f2fs)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4082710Z             printf '# fsck.f2fs -afy'
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4082876Z             flock "$d" fsck.f2fs -afy "$fs"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4083011Z             ;;
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4083142Z         *)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4083422Z             printf "\n\tERROR:   Filesystem checking for '%s' is not\n \
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4083584Z                 available.\n" "$fstype"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4083714Z     esac
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4083851Z }

2026-10-25T03:57:10.4100743Z mountPersistentHome() {
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4100906Z   # support label/uuid
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4101102Z   if [ "${homedev##LABEL=}" != "$homedev" ] ||
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4101287Z     [ "${homedev##UUID=}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4101477Z     homedev=$(blkid -o device -t "$homedev")
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4101760Z   fi
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4101766Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4102028Z   # if we're given a file rather than a blockdev, loop mount it
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4102221Z   if [ "${homedev##mtd}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4102537Z     # mtd devs don't have a block device but get magic-mounted with -t jffs2
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4102694Z     mountopts="-t jffs2"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4102854Z   elif [ ! -b "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4103113Z     if [ "${homedev##/run/initramfs/live}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4103470Z       action "Remounting live store r/w" mount -o remount,rw /run/initramfs/live
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4103601Z     fi
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4103787Z     homedev=$(losetup -f --show "$homedev")
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4103913Z   fi
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4104156Z   homefs=$(blkid -s TYPE -o value "$homedev" 2>/dev/null)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4104162Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4104358Z   # if it's encrypted, we need to unlock it
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4104523Z   if [ "$homefs" = "crypto_LUKS" ]; then
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4104672Z     echo
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4104860Z     echo "Setting up encrypted /home device"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4105261Z     plymouth ask-for-password --command="cryptsetup luksOpen $homedev EncHome"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4105429Z     homedev=/dev/mapper/EncHome
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4105557Z   fi
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4105845Z   if [ "$homedev" ] && [ "${cmdline##* rd.skipfsck[= ]}" = "$cmdline" ]; then
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4106126Z     action "Checking home filesystem" fscheck "$homefs" "$homedev"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4106264Z   fi
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4106271Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4106431Z   # and finally do the mount
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4106636Z   mount ${mountopts:+$mountopts} "$homedev" /home
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4106912Z   # if we have /home under what's passed for persistent home, then
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4107201Z   # we should make that the real /home.  useful for mtd device on olpc
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4107417Z   [ -d /home/home ] && mount --bind /home/home /home
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4107630Z   [ -x /sbin/restorecon ] && /sbin/restorecon /home
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4107812Z   [ -d /home/liveuser ] && USERADDARGS="-M"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4107953Z }

2026-10-25T03:57:10.4108448Z   homedev=${cut%% *}

2026-10-25T03:57:10.3339770Z

2026-10-25T03:57:10.3345978Z     def _is_language_support_installed(self, lang):
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3346769Z         """Is the support for the specified language installed?
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3347386Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3348045Z         The language is considered to be supported if we are not
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3348950Z         able to determine the supported locales due to missing tools.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3349610Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3350011Z         :param lang: a value for the LANG locale variable
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3351200Z         :return: False if the locale is known to be not supported, otherwise True
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3352047Z         """
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3352642Z         try:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3353332Z             output = execWithCapture("locale", ["-a"], root=self._sysroot)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3354178Z         except OSError as e:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3354910Z             log.warning("Couldn't get supported locales: %s", e)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3355687Z             return True
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3356334Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3356801Z         match = find_best_locale_match(lang, output.splitlines())
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3357442Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3357900Z         log.debug("The '%s' locale matched '%s'.", lang, match)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3361198Z         return bool(match)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3361635Z

2026-10-25T03:57:10.3364416Z         """
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3364910Z         try:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3365763Z             fpath = join_paths(self._sysroot, self.LOCALE_CONF_FILE_PATH)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3366682Z             log.debug("Writing the '%s' locale to %s.", lang, fpath)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3367256Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3367570Z             with open(fpath, "w") as fobj:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3368308Z                 fobj.write('LANG="{}"\n'.format(lang))
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3368790Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3369082Z         except OSError as e:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3369859Z             msg = "Cannot write language configuration file: {}".format(e.strerror)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3371160Z             raise LanguageInstallationError(msg) from e

2026-10-25T03:57:10.3579682Z class CompositorLocaledWrapper(LocaledWrapperBase):
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3580582Z     """Localed wrapper class which is used to control compositor.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3581204Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3581613Z     It adds support for layout selection and reactions on the compositor system changes.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3582322Z     """
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3582501Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3582647Z     def __init__(self):
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3583023Z         super().__init__()
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3583283Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3583457Z         self._user_layouts_variants = []
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3583939Z         self._last_layouts_variants = []
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3584247Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3584646Z         self.compositor_layouts_changed = Signal()
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3585242Z         self.compositor_selected_layout_changed = Signal()
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3585635Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3585823Z         # to reflect updates from the compositor
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3586559Z         self._localed_proxy.PropertiesChanged.connect(self._on_properties_changed)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3587115Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3587472Z     def _on_properties_changed(self, interface, changed_props, invalid_props):
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3588286Z         if "X11Layout" in changed_props or "X11Variant" in changed_props:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3589167Z             layouts_variants = self._from_localed_format(changed_props["X11Layout"].get_string(),
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3590035Z                                                          changed_props["X11Variant"].get_string())
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3591531Z             # This part is a bit tricky. The signal processing here means that compositor has
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3592523Z             # changed current layouts configuration. This could happen for multiple reasons:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3593321Z             # - user changed the layout in compositor
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3593871Z             # - Anaconda set the layout to compositor
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3594490Z             # - any other magic logic for compositor (we just don't know)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3595067Z             #
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3595445Z             # The question is how we should behave:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3596162Z             # - we don't want to take compositor layouts to Anaconda because that will change
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3596924Z             #   what user will have in the installed system.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3597669Z             # - we don't want to force our layouts to compositor because that would forbid user
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3598510Z             #   to change compositor layout when Anaconda runs in background
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3599107Z             #
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3599707Z             # The best shot seems to just signal out that the layout has changed and nothing else.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3600288Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3600928Z             # layouts has changed in compositor, always emit this signal
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3601728Z             log.debug("Localed layouts has changed. Last known: '%s' current: '%s'",
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3602483Z                       self._last_layouts_variants, layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3603150Z             self.compositor_layouts_changed.emit(layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3603594Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3603825Z             # check if last selected variant has changed
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3604347Z             # nothing is selected in compositor
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3604890Z             if not layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3605293Z                 log.warning("Compositor layouts not set.")
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3605687Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit("")
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3606199Z             # we don't know last used layouts
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3606736Z             elif not self._last_layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3607342Z                 log.debug("Compositor selected layout is different. "
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3608027Z                           "Missing information about last selected layouts.")
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3608799Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit(layouts_variants[0])
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3609471Z             # selected (first) has changed
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3610065Z             elif layouts_variants[0] != self._last_layouts_variants[0]:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3620986Z                 log.debug("Compositor selected layout is different.")
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3621846Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit(layouts_variants[0])
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3622384Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3622646Z             self._last_layouts_variants = layouts_variants
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3623149Z

2026-10-25T03:57:10.3642948Z     def _set_layouts(self, layouts_variants, options=None, convert=False):
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3643617Z         """Set a new layouts for compositor.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3643943Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3644237Z         Extension of the parent code with compositor specific code.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3644824Z         """
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3645397Z         # If options are not set let's use from a system so we don't change the system settings
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3646117Z         if options is None:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3646540Z             options = self.options
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3646966Z             log.debug(
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3647619Z                 "Keyboard layouts for compositor are missing options. Use compositor options: %s",
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3648375Z                 options,
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3648744Z             )
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3648940Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3649351Z         # Disable keyboard layout switching by keyboard shortcut as it is hard to make this
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3650537Z         # working from the system to localed. The current layout is in general problematic to
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3651459Z         # decide.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3651826Z         log.debug(
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3652549Z             "Disable keyboard layouts switching shortcut to compositor from the options: '%s'",
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3653307Z             options,
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3653651Z         )
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3654146Z         options = list(filter(lambda x: not x.startswith("grp:"), options))
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3654823Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3655004Z         # store configuration from user
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3655572Z         super().set_layouts(layouts_variants, options, convert)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3655989Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3656175Z     def select_layout(self, layout_variant):
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3656751Z         """Select layout from the list of current layouts set.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3657150Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3657514Z         This will search for the given layout variant in the list and move it as first
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3658344Z         in the list. The first layout in systemd is taken as the used one.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3658806Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3659154Z         :param layout_variant: The layout to set, with format "layout (variant)"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3659976Z             (e.g. "cz (qwerty)")
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3660813Z         :type layout_variant: str
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3661354Z         :return: If the keyboard layout was activated
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3661877Z         :rtype: bool
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3662232Z         """
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3662700Z         # ignore compositor layouts but force Anaconda configuration
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3663336Z         layouts = self._user_layouts_variants
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3663677Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3663812Z         try:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3664262Z             new_layouts = self._shift_list(layouts, layout_variant)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3664850Z             self._set_layouts(new_layouts)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3665311Z             return True
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3665688Z         except ValueError:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3666271Z             log.warning("Can't set layout: '%s' as first to the current set: %s",
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3666955Z                         layout_variant, layouts)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3667448Z             return False
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3667683Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3667831Z     @staticmethod
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3668253Z     def _shift_list(source_layouts, value_to_first):
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3668978Z         """Helper method to reorder list of layouts and move one as first in the list.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3669512Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3669883Z         We should preserve the ordering just shift items from start of the list to the
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3670805Z         end in the same order.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3671078Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3671289Z         When we want to set 2nd as first in this list:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3671794Z         ["cz", "es", "us"]
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3672211Z         The result should be:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3672637Z         ["es", "us", "cz"]
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3672896Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3673156Z         So the compositor has the same next layout as Anaconda.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3673584Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3673924Z         :raises: ValueError: if the list is small or the layout is not inside
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3674564Z         """
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3674968Z         value_id = source_layouts.index(value_to_first)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3675744Z         new_list = source_layouts[value_id:len(source_layouts)] + source_layouts[0:value_id]
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3676468Z         return new_list
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3676713Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3676890Z     def select_next_layout(self):
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3677462Z         """Select (make it first) next layout for compositor.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3677880Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3679303Z         Find current compositor layout in the list of defined layouts and set next to it as
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3680722Z         current (first) for compositor. We need to have user defined list because compositor
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3681791Z         layouts will change with the selection. Store this list when user is setting configuration
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3682689Z         to compositor. This list must not change ordering.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3683209Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3683557Z         :param user_layouts: List of layouts selected by user in Anaconda.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3684225Z         :type user_layouts: [str]
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3684990Z         :return: If switch was successful True otherwise False
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3685559Z         :rtype: bool
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3685929Z         """
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3686335Z         current_layout = self.current_layout_variant
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3686865Z         layout_id = 0
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3687099Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3687287Z         if not self._user_layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3688038Z             log.error("Can't switch next layout - user defined keyboard layout is not present!")
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3688777Z             return False
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3689030Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3689185Z         # find next layout
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3689673Z         for i, v in enumerate(self._user_layouts_variants):
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3690231Z             if v == current_layout:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3691130Z                 layout_id = i + 1
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3691620Z                 layout_id %= len(self._user_layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3692004Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3692146Z         try:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3692606Z             new_layouts = self._shift_list(self._user_layouts_variants,
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3693296Z                                            self._user_layouts_variants[layout_id])
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3693872Z             self._set_layouts(new_layouts)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3694318Z             return True
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3694697Z         except ValueError:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3695300Z             log.warning("Can't set next keyboard layout %s", self._user_layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3695977Z             return False

2026-10-25T03:57:10.3900244Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3901017Z def get_missing_keyboard_configuration(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap):
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3901801Z     """Get keyboard configuration if not set by user.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3902170Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3902337Z     Algorithm works as this:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3902847Z     1. Check if full keyboard configuration is already set
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3903392Z        -> return these
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3903902Z     2. If no X layouts, check if they can be obtained from live system
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3904517Z        -> read X layouts
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3904957Z     3a. If still no configuration is present
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3905605Z        -> use DEFAULT_KEYBOARD as console layout and fall through to...
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3906371Z     3b. If only one of the keyboard layout or virtual console keymap is set
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3907036Z        -> convert one to the other by localed
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3907373Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3907694Z     :param localed_wrapper: instance of systemd-localed service wrapper
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3908355Z     :type localed_wrapper: LocaledWrapper
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3908894Z     :param x_layouts: list of X layout specifications
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3909425Z     :type x_layouts: list(str)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3909935Z     :param vc_keymap: virtual console keyboard mapping name
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3910816Z     :type vc_keymap: str
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3911330Z     :returns: tuple of X layouts and VC keyboard settings
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3911905Z     :rtype: (list(str), str))
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3912315Z     """
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3912654Z     if vc_keymap and x_layouts:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3913348Z         log.debug("Keyboard layouts and virtual console keymap already set - nothing to do")
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3914093Z         return x_layouts, vc_keymap
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3914586Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3914789Z     live_keyboard = get_live_keyboard_instance()
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3915545Z     # layouts are not set by user, we should take a look for live configuration if available
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3916277Z     if not x_layouts and live_keyboard:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3916892Z         log.debug("Keyboard configuration from Live system is available")
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3917591Z         x_layouts = live_keyboard.read_keyboard_layouts()
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3917973Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3918142Z     if not vc_keymap and not x_layouts:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3918869Z         log.debug("Using default value %s for missing virtual console keymap", DEFAULT_KEYBOARD)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3919630Z         vc_keymap = DEFAULT_KEYBOARD
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3919926Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3920253Z     if not vc_keymap or not x_layouts:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3921258Z         x_layouts, vc_keymap = _resolve_missing_by_conversion(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3921901Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3922061Z     return x_layouts, vc_keymap
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3922337Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3922344Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3922689Z def _resolve_missing_by_conversion(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap):
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3923339Z     if not vc_keymap:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3923813Z         vc_keymap = localed_wrapper.convert_layouts(x_layouts)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3924588Z         log.debug("Missing virtual console keymap value %s converted from %s X layouts",
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3925337Z                   vc_keymap, x_layouts)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3925804Z     if not x_layouts:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3926096Z         x_layouts = localed_wrapper.convert_keymap(vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3926553Z         log.debug("Missing X layouts value %s converted from %s virtual console keymap",
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3926980Z                   x_layouts, vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3927159Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3927258Z     return x_layouts, vc_keymap

2026-10-25T03:57:10.3947198Z # Run a local bridge, web server, and browser for a particular Cockpit page.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3948097Z # This is useful for integration into .desktop files, for systems which don't
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3949020Z # have cockpit.socket enabled. The web server and browser run in an unshared
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3949904Z # network namespace, and thus are totally isolated from everything else.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3951557Z #
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3951896Z # Usage:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3952531Z #     cockpit-desktop /cockpit/@localhost/system/index.html -t default -r 0
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3953393Z # For testing purposes one can also make cockpit-ws bind to all addresses
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3954160Z # (this is insecure and should not be used in prodcuction):
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3954917Z #     cockpit-desktop /cockpit/@localhost/system/index.html -t default -r 1
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3955549Z #
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3955850Z set -eu
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3956032Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3956183Z THEME_ID="default"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3956536Z WEBUI_REMOTE=0
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3956888Z while getopts t:r: option
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3957277Z do
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3957607Z     case "${option}"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3957983Z         in
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3958339Z         t)THEME_ID=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3958798Z         r)WEBUI_REMOTE=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3959347Z         *) echo "Usage: $0 [-t THEME_ID] [-r WEBUI_REMOTE]" >&2
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3959897Z        exit 1 ;;
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3960232Z     esac
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3960775Z done
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3960955Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3961211Z URL_PATH="/cockpit/@localhost/anaconda-webui/index.html"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3961641Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3961803Z WEBUI_ADDRESS="127.0.0.1"

2026-10-25T03:57:10.3964867Z # Function to setup user-related environment
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3965365Z setup_user_environment() {
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3965908Z     # PKEXEC_UID is the uid for "gnome-initial-setup" or "liveuser"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3966561Z     # depending on how the installer gets started.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3967057Z     #
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3967577Z     # It's unset on non-live-images, so we just use the current user then (root)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3968252Z     if [ -n "${PKEXEC_UID:-}" ]; then
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3968797Z       # If PKEXEC_UID is set, use the user associated with it
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3969396Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(id -n -u "${PKEXEC_UID}")
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3969877Z     else
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3970898Z       # Try to find another user who is not root and has /usr/bin/bash as their shell
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3971963Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(grep '/usr/bin/bash' /etc/passwd | grep -v '^root' | cut -d: -f1 | head -n 1)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3972594Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3972900Z       # If no such user is found, use the current user running the script
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3973538Z       if [ -z "${INSTALLER_USER:-}" ]; then
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3974019Z         INSTALLER_USER=$(id -un)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3974427Z       fi
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3974732Z     fi
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3974926Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3975131Z     # Get the home directory of the selected user
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3975762Z     HOME_DIR=$(grep "^$INSTALLER_USER:" /etc/passwd | cut -d: -f6)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3976380Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3976639Z     # Check if the home directory exists, otherwise create it
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3977210Z     if [ ! -d "$HOME_DIR" ]; then
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3977795Z       echo "The home directory $HOME_DIR does not exist. Creating it." >&2
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3978426Z       mkdir -p "$HOME_DIR"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3978843Z       chown "$INSTALLER_USER" "$HOME_DIR"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3979286Z     else
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3979687Z       echo "Home directory $HOME_DIR already exists." >&2
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3980199Z     fi
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3980597Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3980839Z     # Return the user and home directory for further use
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3981385Z     echo "$INSTALLER_USER"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3981771Z }

2026-10-25T03:57:10.3982263Z get_default_browser() {
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3983005Z     # Read the default browser from the anaconda runtime configuration file but wait till it's available
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3983992Z     # FIXME: Related to: https://issues.redhat.com/browse/INSTALLER-4223
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3984629Z     timeout=60
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3984969Z     elapsed=0
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3985167Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3985380Z     while [ ! -f /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf ]; do
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3985929Z         if [ "$elapsed" -ge "$timeout" ]; then
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3986590Z             echo "Timed out waiting for anaconda.conf to be available." >&2
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3987206Z             exit 1
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3987418Z         fi
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3987620Z         sleep 1
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3987846Z         elapsed=$((elapsed + 1))
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3988308Z     done
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3988508Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3988982Z     # FIXME: Should be provided as an argument to this script instead of grepping the config file
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3990049Z     DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(grep '^webui_web_engine =' /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf | cut -d'=' -f2)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3991254Z     if [ -n "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" ]; then
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3991895Z         echo "Default browser set in anaconda.conf: $DEFAULT_BROWSER" >&2
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3992751Z         DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" | sed 's/^[[:space:]]*//;s/[[:space:]]*$//')
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3993496Z         echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3993923Z     else
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3994441Z         echo "No default browser set in anaconda.conf, using firefox." >&2
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3995086Z         echo "firefox"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3995461Z     fi
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3995778Z }

2026-10-25T03:57:10.3996113Z prepare_firefox_cmd() {
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3996513Z     INSTALLER_USER=$1
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3996748Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3997125Z     # prepare empty firefox profile dir with theme based on the passed profile id
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3997925Z     FIREFOX_THEME_DIR="/usr/share/anaconda/firefox-theme"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3998702Z     FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH="${XDG_RUNTIME_DIR}/anaconda/firefox-profile"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3999230Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.3999756Z     # make sure the profile directory exists and is empty
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4000628Z     if [ -d ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH} ]
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4001116Z     then
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4001649Z         echo "Cleaning up existing Anaconda Firefox profile directory." >&2
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4002322Z         rm -rf ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4002816Z     fi
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4003339Z     pkexec --user "${INSTALLER_USER}" mkdir -p ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4003827Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4004135Z     # populate the profile directory with our custom Firefox theme
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4004992Z     # - theme id is passed as the second argument of this script
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4005660Z     THEME_PATH="${FIREFOX_THEME_DIR}/${THEME_ID}"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4006056Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4006447Z     pkexec --user "${INSTALLER_USER}" cp -a "${THEME_PATH}/." ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}

2026-10-25T03:57:10.4037102Z   # If not, create a dummy function that returns the input verbatim

2026-10-25T03:57:10.4038461Z

2026-10-25T03:57:10.4039308Z strstr() {

2026-10-25T03:57:10.4040008Z }

2026-10-25T03:57:10.4040566Z # Run some action. Log its output.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4041026Z action() {
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4041357Z     local STRING rc
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4041575Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4041714Z     STRING=$1
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4042079Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4042532Z     shift
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4042841Z     if "$@"; then
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4043213Z         success "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4043656Z     else
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4043990Z         failure "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4044603Z     fi
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4044913Z     rc=$?
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4045223Z     printf '\n'
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4045572Z     return $rc
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4045917Z }
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4046096Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4046381Z # verbose ->> very (very!) old bootup look (prior to RHL-6.0?)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4046973Z # color ->> default bootup look
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4047531Z # other ->> default bootup look without ANSI colors or positioning
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4048111Z BOOTUP=color
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4048469Z # Column to start "[  OK  ]" label in:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4048907Z RES_COL=60
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4049269Z # terminal sequence to move to that column:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4049758Z MOVE_TO_COL="printf \\033[${RES_COL}G"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4050783Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'success' (bright green):
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4051445Z SETCOLOR_SUCCESS="printf \\033[1;32m"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4052045Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'failure' (bright red):
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4052743Z SETCOLOR_FAILURE="printf \\033[1;31m"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4053336Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'warning' (bright yellow):
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4053948Z SETCOLOR_WARNING="printf \\033[1;33m"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4054473Z # Terminal sequence to reset to the default color:
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4055016Z SETCOLOR_NORMAL="printf \\033[0;39m"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4055325Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4055493Z # NOTE: /dev/ttyS* is serial console.
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4056088Z # 'not a tty' is reported when executed under systemd service units
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4056821Z # where /dev/null is associated with standard input
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4057234Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4057419Z test_tty=$(LC_ALL=C tty)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4058056Z case "$test_tty" in
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4058464Z     /dev/ttyS* | 'not a tty')
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4058901Z         BOOTUP=serial
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4059282Z         MOVE_TO_COL=
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4059658Z         SETCOLOR_SUCCESS=
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4060063Z         SETCOLOR_FAILURE=
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4060696Z         SETCOLOR_WARNING=
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4061130Z         SETCOLOR_NORMAL=
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4061528Z         ;;
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4061854Z esac
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4062055Z

2026-10-25T03:57:10.4064180Z

2026-10-25T03:57:10.4065503Z     [ "$BOOTUP" != "verbose" ] && echo_failure
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4066061Z     [ -x /bin/plymouth ] && /bin/plymouth --details
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4066577Z     return $rc
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4066905Z }
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4067079Z

2026-10-25T03:57:10.4069743Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_NORMAL
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4070224Z     printf '%s' "]"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4071205Z     printf '\r'
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4071553Z     return 0
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4071873Z }
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4072041Z

2026-10-25T03:57:10.4100740Z mountPersistentHome() {
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4100903Z   # support label/uuid
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4101098Z   if [ "${homedev##LABEL=}" != "$homedev" ] ||
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4101283Z     [ "${homedev##UUID=}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4101474Z     homedev=$(blkid -o device -t "$homedev")
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4101757Z   fi
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4101764Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4102024Z   # if we're given a file rather than a blockdev, loop mount it
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4102212Z   if [ "${homedev##mtd}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4102534Z     # mtd devs don't have a block device but get magic-mounted with -t jffs2
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4102690Z     mountopts="-t jffs2"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4102851Z   elif [ ! -b "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4103110Z     if [ "${homedev##/run/initramfs/live}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4103466Z       action "Remounting live store r/w" mount -o remount,rw /run/initramfs/live
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4103598Z     fi
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4103784Z     homedev=$(losetup -f --show "$homedev")
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4103911Z   fi
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4104152Z   homefs=$(blkid -s TYPE -o value "$homedev" 2>/dev/null)
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4104160Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4104354Z   # if it's encrypted, we need to unlock it
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4104520Z   if [ "$homefs" = "crypto_LUKS" ]; then
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4104668Z     echo
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4104857Z     echo "Setting up encrypted /home device"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4105251Z     plymouth ask-for-password --command="cryptsetup luksOpen $homedev EncHome"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4105426Z     homedev=/dev/mapper/EncHome
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4105554Z   fi
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4105842Z   if [ "$homedev" ] && [ "${cmdline##* rd.skipfsck[= ]}" = "$cmdline" ]; then
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4106122Z     action "Checking home filesystem" fscheck "$homefs" "$homedev"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4106261Z   fi
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4106268Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4106428Z   # and finally do the mount
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4106633Z   mount ${mountopts:+$mountopts} "$homedev" /home
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4106909Z   # if we have /home under what's passed for persistent home, then
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4107197Z   # we should make that the real /home.  useful for mtd device on olpc
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4107414Z   [ -d /home/home ] && mount --bind /home/home /home
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4107627Z   [ -x /sbin/restorecon ] && /sbin/restorecon /home
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4107809Z   [ -d /home/liveuser ] && USERADDARGS="-M"
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4107945Z }
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4107958Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4108131Z cut=${cmdline##* persistenthome[= ]}
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4108299Z if [ ${#cut} -ne ${#cmdline} ]; then
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4108445Z   homedev=${cut%% *}
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4108687Z elif [ -e /run/initramfs/live/"${livedir}"/home.img ]; then
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4108894Z   homedev=/run/initramfs/live/"${livedir}"/home.img
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4109029Z fi
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4109036Z
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4109341Z # if we have a persistent /home, then we want to go ahead and mount it
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4109623Z { ! strstr "$cmdline" nopersistenthome && [ "$homedev" ]; } &&
2026-10-25T03:57:10.4109891Z   action "Mounting persistent /home" mountPersistentHome
